```python
"""
Step-function search for the polynomial-square peak ratio.

The objective is scale-invariant:
    score(a) = 2 * n * max(a*a) / (sum(a)^2)
For a normalized nonnegative sequence q with sum 1, minimize max(q*q).

This program combines several complementary methods:
  1) Direct polynomial-square-root initializations of an approximately flat
     target square.
  2) Time-domain multiplicative feedback descent driven by the current peak
     weights of q*q.
  3) L-BFGS minimization of a log-sum-exp smoothed maximum, parameterized by
     log-softmax variables for stability and nonnegativity.
  4) Frequency-domain projection onto a box-constrained target for the square,
     followed by polynomial square root and nonnegativity projection.
  5) Spectral amplitude flattening as an additional escape mechanism.

The best sequence found is returned.  The final answer is optionally checked
against the globally available evaluate_sequence() when present.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def safe_score_sequence(seq_list):
    """Call the external evaluator safely."""
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative, enforce sum 1, and return float64 array or None."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return None
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def softmax_vec(z):
    """Stable softmax."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=np.float64) / len(z)
    return e / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a by FFT; sufficient for n >= 2."""
    n = int(len(a))
    M = 2 * n - 1
    if n < 64:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:M]


def conv_score(a):
    """Fast internal score equivalent to evaluate_sequence for nonnegative a."""
    q = normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)
    if q is None:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(q)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return 2.0 * len(q) * M


def sequences_close(a, b, tol=1e-10):
    """Cheap near-equality test for duplicate candidates."""
    if len(a) != len(b):
        return False
    if np.any(~np.isfinite(a)) or np.any(~np.isfinite(b)):
        return False
    return float(np.linalg.norm(np.asarray(a) - np.asarray(b))) < tol


def resample_to_length(a, n_new):
    """Linear resample to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = len(a)
    if m == 0:
        return None
    if m == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def make_initializations(n, rng, parent):
    """Produce diverse nonnegative initial sequences."""
    inits = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_to_length(parent, n)
        if p is not None:
            q = normalize_nonneg(p, floor=0.0)
            if q is not None:
                inits.append(q)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = idx + 0.5
    right = n - idx + 0.5

    # Edge singular / U-shaped candidates.
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
        q = np.power(left, -alpha) * np.power(right, -alpha)
        qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
        if qq is not None:
            inits.append(qq)

    # Asymmetric U-shapes.
    for a, b in ((0.75, 0.25), (0.25, 0.75), (1.0, 0.5), (0.5, 1.0)):
        q = np.power(left, -a) * np.power(right, -b)
        qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
        if qq is not None:
            inits.append(qq)

    x = idx / max(1.0, n - 1.0)

    q = np.maximum(0.1 + 0.9 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0), 1e-3)
    qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
    if qq is not None:
        inits.append(qq)

    q = np.maximum(0.2 + 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * x) ** 2, 1e-3)
    qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
    if qq is not None:
        inits.append(qq)

    q = np.maximum(0.3 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) ** 2, 1e-3)
    qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
    if qq is not None:
        inits.append(qq)

    # Random smooth positive candidates.
    for _ in range(3):
        q = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.45
        for f in range(1, 6):
            amp = 0.05 * rng.random()
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            q += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / n + ph)
        qq = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=0.0)
        if qq is not None:
            inits.append(qq)

    return inits


def random_sqrt_init(n, rng, n_trials=6):
    """
    Randomized square-root initialization of a perfectly flat square target.

    If T is the flat length-(2n-1) sequence summing to 1, then T = b*b in the
    frequency domain means B = sqrt(DFT(T)).  Random Hermitian sign choices give
    valid square roots; projecting first n time samples and clipping yields a
    feasible starting point.
    """
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    target = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / M
    Cf = np.fft.fft(target, L)
    Bf = np.sqrt(Cf)

    inv = np.arange(L)
    Bf = 0.5 * (Bf + np.conj(Bf[(-inv) % L]))
    Bf[0] = float(abs(Bf[0]))
    if L % 2 == 0:
        Bf[L // 2] = float(abs(Bf[L // 2]))

    best_q = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    for trial in range(max(1, int(n_trials))):
        signs = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if trial > 0:
            half = L // 2
            s_half = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64), size=half)
            idx = np.arange(1, half + 1)
            signs[idx] = s_half
            signs[L - idx] = s_half
            if L % 2 == 0:
                signs[half] = 1.0

        b = np.fft.ifft(Bf * signs).real
        cand = np.maximum(b[:n], 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s > 1e-8:
            qq = cand / s
            sc = conv_score(qq)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_q = qq.copy()

    if best_q is None:
        best_q = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return best_q


def water_fill_box(c, T):
    """
    Project a nonnegative vector c with sum 1 onto {sum 1, 0 <= x <= T}
    by capping at T and redistributing deficit uniformly among uncapped entries.
    """
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return c
    c = c / s
    T = float(max(T, 0.0))
    if T <= 0.0:
        return c
    if float(c.max()) <= T:
        return c

    out = np.minimum(c, T)
    for _ in range(32):
        rem = 1.0 - float(out.sum())
        if rem <= 1e-12:
            break
        low = out < T
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(low))
        if cnt <= 0:
            break
        add = rem / cnt
        if add <= 0.0:
            break
        max_low = float(out[low].max())
        if max_low + add <= T:
            out[low] += add
            break
        threshold = T - add
        fix = (out > threshold) & (out < T)
        if not np.any(fix):
            low_idx = np.where(low)[0]
            if low_idx.size == 0:
                break
            idx = int(low_idx[np.argmax(out[low_idx])])
            fix = np.zeros_like(out, dtype=bool)
            fix[idx] = True
        out[fix] = T

    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s2 = float(out.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        out = out / s2
    return out


def _hermitian_sqrt(based):
    """Robustly take a Hermitian-consistent square root of a real DFT."""
    L = len(based)
    Bf = np.sqrt(based)
    inv = np.arange(L)
    Bf = 0.5 * (Bf + np.conj(Bf[(-inv) % L]))
    Bf[0] = float(abs(Bf[0]))
    if L % 2 == 0:
        Bf[L // 2] = float(abs(Bf[L // 2]))
    return Bf


def box_sqrt_step(q, T):
    """
    One direct square-root projection step:
      current square -> box-constrained target -> square root -> nonnegative time.
    """
    q = normalize_nonneg(q, floor=1e-14)
    if q is None:
        return None
    n = len(q)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    c = fast_conv(q)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    c = c / s
    T = max(float(T), 1.001 / M)
    target = water_fill_box(c, T)

    L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    Cf = np.fft.fft(target, L)
    Bf = _hermitian_sqrt(Cf)
    b = np.fft.ifft(Bf).real
    cand = np.maximum(b[:n], 0.0)
    ss = float(cand.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-8:
        return None
    return cand / ss


def feedback_descent(q0, time_limit, beta_min=1000.0, beta_max=1000000.0,
                     alpha_min=0.2, alpha_max=1.0, rng=None):
    """
    Multiplicative time-domain feedback descent.

    For peak weights u, define feedback F_j = sum_k u_k q_{k-j}.  High-feedback
    variables are damped, low-feedback variables are boosted, then normalized.
    """
    q = normalize_nonneg(q0, floor=1e-14)
    if q is None:
        return None, np.inf

    n = len(q)
    best = q.copy()
    current = q.copy()
    current_sc = conv_score(q)
    best_sc = current_sc
    M_lower = 1.0005 / (2 * n - 1)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240501)

    start = time.monotonic()
    duration = max(1e-6, float(time_limit - start))
    no_accept = 0

    while time.monotonic() < time_limit:
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / duration)

        if beta_max > beta_min:
            beta = beta_min * ((beta_max / beta_min) ** progress)
        else:
            beta = beta_min

        alpha = alpha_min + (alpha_max - alpha_min) * progress
        if no_accept > 0:
            alpha = min(alpha_max, max(alpha_min, alpha * (0.7 if no_accept < 30 else 1.25)))

        c = fast_conv(q)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
        if sc < current_sc:
            current_sc = sc
            current = q.copy()
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = q.copy()

        if float(c.max()) <= M_lower:
            break

        scaled = beta * (c - float(c.max()))
        expv = np.exp(scaled)
        s_exp = float(expv.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s_exp) or s_exp <= 0.0:
            break
        u = expv / s_exp

        Mconv = 3 * n - 2
        L2 = 1 << (Mconv - 1).bit_length()
        Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L2)
        Ur = np.fft.rfft(u[::-1], L2)
        full = np.fft.irfft(Q * Ur, L2)
        corr = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

        pos = corr > 1e-300
        if np.any(pos):
            med_corr = float(np.median(corr[pos]))
        else:
            med_corr = float(np.max(corr))
        avg_corr = float(np.dot(u, c))
        C = max(0.5 * (med_corr + avg_corr), 1e-300)

        factor = (C / np.maximum(corr, 1e-300)) ** alpha
        factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-3, 1e3)
        q_raw = normalize_nonneg(q * factor, floor=0.0)
        if q_raw is None:
            break

        accepted = False
        for blend in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            cand = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - blend) * q + blend * q_raw, floor=0.0)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc_c = conv_score(cand)
            if sc_c < current_sc - 1e-14:
                q = cand.copy()
                current = q.copy()
                current_sc = sc_c
                accepted = True
                if sc_c < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_c
                    best = q.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 35:
                q = normalize_nonneg(best * (1.0 + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)), floor=1e-14)
                if q is None:
                    break
                current_sc = conv_score(q)
                current = q.copy()
                no_accept = 0
            if no_accept > 150:
                break

    if conv_score(q) < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(conv_score(q))
        best = q.copy()
    return best, best_sc


def lse_softmax_objective(z, beta):
    """
    Smoothed exact score with log-softmax parameterization.

    Values:
       L_beta = logsumexp(beta * (q*q)) / beta
       score  = 2 n L_beta

    Gradient is analytic.
    """
    n = len(z)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)

    zc = z - float(np.mean(z))
    q = softmax_vec(zc)

    c = fast_conv(q)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    r = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = beta * (c - r)
    expv = np.exp(scaled)
    exps = float(expv.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(exps) or exps <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)

    u = expv / exps
    L = r + float(np.log(exps)) / beta
    val = 2.0 * n * L

    Mconv = 3 * n - 2
    L2 = 1 << (Mconv - 1).bit_length()
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L2)
    Ur = np.fft.rfft(u[::-1], L2)
    full = np.fft.irfft(Q * Ur, L2)
    corr = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    avg = float(np.dot(u, c))
    grad = 2.0 * q * (corr - avg)
    grad *= 4.0 * n
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    return val, grad


def estimate_lse_cost(n, beta=20000.0):
    """Rough wall time of one LSE objective gradient evaluation."""
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lse_softmax_objective(z, beta)
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    return max(1e-5, float(dt))


def lbfgs_stage(q0, beta, time_limit, est_cost, maxfun_cap=2500):
    """Run a bounded-by-time L-BFGS stage on the LSE objective."""
    q = normalize_nonneg(q0, floor=1e-14)
    if q is None:
        return q0.copy()

    z = np.log(q)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    remaining = max(0.5, float(time_limit - time.monotonic()))
    maxfun = int(remaining / max(1e-6, est_cost * 1.5))
    maxfun = int(np.clip(maxfun, 30, maxfun_cap))
    maxiter = int(np.clip(maxfun, 30, 500))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_softmax_objective,
            z,
            args=(beta,),
            method="L-BFGS",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxcor": 20,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
        )
        z_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if len(z_out) != len(z) or not np.all(np.isfinite(z_out)):
            return q
        z_out -= float(np.mean(z_out))
        q_out = softmax_vec(z_out)
        sc0 = conv_score(q)
        sc1 = conv_score(q_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < sc0:
            return q_out
        return q
    except Exception:
        return q


def amp_flat_optimize(q0, time_limit, alpha_min=0.05, alpha_max=0.85):
    """
    Spectral amplitude flattening in the autospectrum (Fienup-style), with
    acceptance by exact peak score.
    """
    q = normalize_nonneg(q0, floor=1e-14)
    if q is None:
        return None, np.inf

    n = len(q)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()

    best = q.copy()
    current_sc = conv_score(q)
    best_sc = current_sc
    alpha = 0.3 * (alpha_min + alpha_max)
    no_improve = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    duration = max(1e-6, float(time_limit - start))

    while time.monotonic() < time_limit:
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / duration)
        alpha_use = alpha_min + (alpha_max - alpha_min) * progress
        if no_improve > 0:
            alpha_use = min(alpha_max, max(alpha_min * 0.25, alpha_use * 0.7))

        A = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
        amp = np.sqrt(np.maximum(A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag, 0.0))
        phase = np.angle(A)
        if amp.size > 1:
            C = float(np.median(amp[1:]))
        else:
            C = float(amp[0])
        C = max(C, 1e-20)

        amp_safe = np.maximum(amp, 1e-20)
        amp_new = amp_safe * (C / amp_safe) ** alpha_use
        A_new = amp_new * np.exp(1j * phase)
        q_raw = np.fft.irfft(A_new, L)[:n]
        q_raw = normalize_nonneg(q_raw, floor=0.0)
        if q_raw is None:
            break

        accepted = False
        for blend in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            cand = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - blend) * q + blend * q_raw, floor=0.0)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = conv_score(cand)
            if sc < current_sc - 1e-14:
                q = cand.copy()
                current_sc = sc
                accepted = True
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = q.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                break

    return best, best_sc


def box_sqrt_optimize(q0, time_limit, target_score=1.5027, rng=None):
    """
    Escape/polish by annealing the peak box bound T and taking polynomial
    square-root projections of the capped square.
    """
    q = normalize_nonneg(q0, floor=1e-14)
    if q is None:
        return None, np.inf
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(731)

    n = len(q)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    best = q.copy()
    current = q.copy()
    current_sc = conv_score(q)
    best_sc = current_sc
    M0 = current_sc / (2.0 * n)

    T_target = target_score / (2.0 * n)
    T_abs = 1.001 / M
    T_lower = max(T_abs, min(T_target, 0.98 * M0))
    T = min(0.995 * M0, max(T_lower, 0.90 * M0))
    if T >= M0:
        T = 0.9 * M0
    T = max(T, T_abs)

    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < time_limit:
        base = box_sqrt_step(q, T)
        if base is None:
            T = max(T_lower, 0.97 * T)
            continue

        improved = False
        for blend in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            cand = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - blend) * q + blend * base, floor=0.0)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = conv_score(cand)
            if sc < current_sc - 1e-14:
                q = cand.copy()
                current = q.copy()
                current_sc = sc
                improved = True
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = q.copy()
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            T = max(T_lower, 0.999 * T)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            T = max(T_lower, 0.97 * T)
            if no_improve > 18:
                q = normalize_nonneg(best * (1.0 + 0.035 * rng.standard_normal(n)), floor=1e-14)
                if q is None:
                    break
                current = q.copy()
                current_sc = conv_score(q)
                no_improve = 0

        if T <= T_lower * (1.0 + 1e-8) and no_improve > 60:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def improve_sequence(q0, total_time, rng):
    """"""
    full_budget = max(1.0, float(total_time))
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, full_budget - 5.0)

    q0 = normalize_nonneg(q0, floor=1e-14)
    if q0 is None:
        return None, np.inf

    current = q0.copy()
    current_sc = conv_score(q0)
    best = q0.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc
    n = len(q0)

    try:
        est_cost = estimate_lse_cost(n)
    except Exception:
        est_cost = 0.02

    # Phase 1: broad multiplicative feedback.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phase_end = min(deadline, start + 0.25 * full_budget)
        try:
            new_q, new_sc = feedback_descent(
                current, phase_end, beta_min=200.0, beta_max=25000.0,
                alpha_min=0.10, alpha_max=0.75, rng=rng)
        except Exception:
            new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
        if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                current = new_q.copy()
                current_sc = new_sc
            if new_sc < best_sc:
                best = new_q.copy()
                best_sc = new_sc

    # Phase 2: moderate L-BFGS smoothing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phase_end = min(deadline, start + 0.45 * full_budget)
        for beta in (5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= phase_end:
                break
            remaining_phase = max(0.0, phase_end - time.monotonic())
            stage_budget = min(remaining_phase, max(8.0, remaining_phase / 3.0))
            try:
                new_q = lbfgs_stage(current, beta, time.monotonic() + stage_budget, est_cost)
                new_sc = conv_score(new_q)
            except Exception:
                new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
            if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
                if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                    current = new_q.copy()
                    current_sc = new_sc
                if new_sc < best_sc:
                    best = new_q.copy()
                    best_sc = new_sc

    # Phase 3: sharper feedback.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phase_end = min(deadline, start + 0.65 * full_budget)
        try:
            new_q, new_sc = feedback_descent(
                current, phase_end, beta_min=10000.0, beta_max=600000.0,
                alpha_min=0.25, alpha_max=1.00, rng=rng)
        except Exception:
            new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
        if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                current = new_q.copy()
                current_sc = new_sc
            if new_sc < best_sc:
                best = new_q.copy()
                best_sc = new_sc

    # Phase 4: high-beta L-BFGS polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phase_end = min(deadline, start + 0.80 * full_budget)
        for beta in (120000.0, 450000.0, 1600000.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= phase_end:
                break
            remaining_phase = max(0.0, phase_end - time.monotonic())
            stage_budget = min(remaining_phase, max(6.0, remaining_phase / 3.0))
            try:
                new_q = lbfgs_stage(current, beta, time.monotonic() + stage_budget, est_cost)
                new_sc = conv_score(new_q)
            except Exception:
                new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
            if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
                if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                    current = new_q.copy()
                    current_sc = new_sc
                if new_sc < best_sc:
                    best = new_q.copy()
                    best_sc = new_sc

    # Phase 5: projection escapes and final short feedback.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phase_end = min(deadline, start + 0.95 * full_budget)
        try:
            new_q, new_sc = box_sqrt_optimize(
                current, min(phase_end, time.monotonic() + max(6.0, 0.45 * (phase_end - time.monotonic()))),
                target_score=1.5027, rng=rng)
        except Exception:
            new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
        if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                current = new_q.copy()
                current_sc = new_sc
            if new_sc < best_sc:
                best = new_q.copy()
                best_sc = new_sc

        if time.monotonic() < phase_end:
            try:
                new_q, new_sc = amp_flat_optimize(current, phase_end)
            except Exception:
                new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
            if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
                if new_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                    current = new_q.copy()
                    current_sc = new_sc
                if new_sc < best_sc:
                    best = new_q.copy()
                    best_sc = new_sc

    # Final exact feedback if any time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            new_q, new_sc = feedback_descent(
                current, deadline, beta_min=150000.0, beta_max=2500000.0,
                alpha_min=0.10, alpha_max=0.85, rng=rng)
        except Exception:
            new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
        if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < best_sc:
                best = new_q.copy()
                best_sc = new_sc

    if conv_score(best) < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(conv_score(best))
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Entry point: search for the best nonnegative sequence and return it as a list.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    safety = 90.0 if total_budget > 300.0 else 0.1 * total_budget
    deadline = t_start + max(20.0, total_budget - safety)

    # Load previous construction, if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 16:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    parent_actual = np.inf
    parent_list = None
    if parent is not None and parent.size <= 20000:
        parent_list = [float(x) for x in parent]
        pa = safe_score_sequence(parent_list)
        if np.isfinite(pa):
            parent_actual = pa
        else:
            parent_list = None
            parent_actual = np.inf
    else:
        parent_list = None

    # Safe baseline length.
    if parent is not None and 1 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        primary_n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        primary_n = 8192
    primary_n = int(max(128, min(primary_n, 20000)))

    best_array = None
    best_internal = np.inf

    parent_resized = None
    parent_norm = None
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size == primary_n:
            parent_resized = parent.copy()
        else:
            parent_resized = resample_to_length(parent, primary_n)
        if parent_resized is not None:
            parent_norm = normalize_nonneg(parent_resized, floor=0.0)
            if parent_norm is not None:
                sc = conv_score(parent_norm)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_internal:
                    best_internal = sc
                    best_array = parent_norm.copy()

    print("Start search")
    print(f"  parent available: {parent is not None}, primary_n={primary_n}, "
          f"parent_actual={parent_actual:.10f}")

    # Build initial candidates.
    init_arrays = make_initializations(primary_n, rng, None)
    if parent_resized is not None:
        init_arrays.append(parent_resized.copy())
    if total_budget > 200.0:
        try:
            init_arrays.append(random_sqrt_init(primary_n, rng, n_trials=5))
        except Exception:
            pass

    init_list = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(init_arrays):
        q = normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)
        if q is None:
            continue
        sc = conv_score(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            init_list.append((sc, idx, q))
    init_list.sort()

    candidates = []
    if parent_norm is not None:
        candidates.append(parent_norm.copy())
    for sc, idx, q in init_list:
        if len(candidates) >= 4:
            break
        dup = False
        for c in candidates:
            if sequences_close(c, q, 1e-12):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            candidates.append(q.copy())

    if not candidates:
        candidates.append(np.ones(primary_n, dtype=np.float64) / primary_n)

    print(f"  number of candidate families: {len(candidates)}")

    fractions = (0.40, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05)
    for i, cand in enumerate(candidates):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 70.0:
            break
        frac = fractions[min(i, len(fractions) - 1)]
        budget = remaining * frac
        if budget < 60.0:
            continue

        print(f"  optimizing candidate {i}: initial score={conv_score(cand):.10f}, budget={budget:.0f}s")
        try:
            new_q, new_sc = improve_sequence(cand, budget, rng)
        except Exception:
            new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
        if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < best_internal:
                best_internal = new_sc
                best_array = new_q.copy()
                print(f"    NEW BEST: {best_internal:.10f} (n={len(best_array)}, "
                      f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s)")

    # Optional longer-length experiment if time remains.
    if best_array is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 450.0:
        alt_n = 12288
        if alt_n != primary_n and alt_n <= 20000:
            alt_candidates = []
            try:
                alt_candidates.append(random_sqrt_init(alt_n, rng, n_trials=6))
            except Exception:
                pass
            if parent is not None:
                p_alt = resample_to_length(parent, alt_n)
                if p_alt is not None:
                    p_alt = normalize_nonneg(p_alt, floor=0.0)
                    if p_alt is not None:
                        alt_candidates.append(p_alt)

            for alt_c in alt_candidates:
                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if remaining < 300.0:
                    break
                budget = min(450.0, remaining * 0.35)
                if budget < 90.0:
                    break
                acand = normalize_nonneg(alt_c, floor=0.0)
                if acand is None:
                    continue
                print(f"  alt length experiment: n={alt_n}, budget={budget:.0f}s")
                try:
                    new_q, new_sc = improve_sequence(acand, budget, rng)
                except Exception:
                    new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
                if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
                    if new_sc < best_internal:
                        best_internal = new_sc
                        best_array = new_q.copy()
                        print(f"    ALT NEW BEST: {best_internal:.10f} (n={len(best_array)}, "
                              f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s}")

    # Final polish on the global best.
    if best_array is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        final_budget = min(300.0, remaining * 0.6)
        if final_budget > 40.0:
            print(f"  final polish budget={final_budget:.0f}s")
            try:
                new_q, new_sc = improve_sequence(best_array, final_budget, rng)
            except Exception:
                new_q, new_sc = None, np.inf
            if new_q is not None and np.isfinite(new_sc):
                if new_sc < best_internal:
                    best_internal = new_sc
                    best_array = new_q.copy()
                    print(f"    FINAL NEW BEST: {best_internal:.10f} (n={len(best_array)})")

    # Prepare a guaranteed valid output.
    if best_array is None:
        out_arr = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
    else:
        out_arr = np.asarray(best_array, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(np.sum(out_arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
        s = float(np.sum(out_arr))
    out_arr = out_arr / s
    out_list = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    verified = safe_score_sequence(out_list)

    # If the verified answer is worse than the known previous sequence, fall back.
    final_list = out_list
    if parent_list is not None and np.isfinite(parent_actual):
        if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified > parent_actual + 1e-8:
            final_list = parent_list

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal best = {best_internal:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified best = {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"Length = {len(final_list)}, elapsed = {elapsed:.1f}s / {total_budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return final_list
```
